BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                       董事會成員多元化政策
The Board has adopted a Board diversity policy which sets out the            董事會採納董事會成員多元化政策，該政策載列

of risk management, connecting the channels of communication of risk        接各上下級、各部門和業務單位的風險管理資訊

MATERIAL RISKS THE GROUP MAY FACE                                           本集團可能面臨的重大風險

The material risks that the Group may face which include macro-             本集團可能面臨的重大風險包括宏觀經濟風險、
economic risks, risks of inappropriate strategies for market                市場競爭策略不當風險、原材料價格波動風險、
competition, risks of the fluctuation of raw materials price, risks of      環保政策、經濟政策與行業政策調整風險、網路
adjustments of policies in relation to the environmental protection,        安全風險、2019 冠狀病毒病（「COVID-19」）風險以
economy and industry, cybersecurity risks, risks of the coronavirus         及氣候變化風險。
disease (“COVID-19”), and climate change risks.

The potential risks of macro-economic risks arise from the effects of       宏觀經濟風險的潛在風險產生自宏觀經濟波動的
macro-economy’s volatility and the pressure of inflation. The Group’s       影響及通貨膨脹的壓力。本集團風險應對措施包
corresponding measures on risk management include reviewing the             括檢討宏觀經濟風險的管理策略和管理機制；檢
management strategies and mechanism for macro-economic risks;               討收集外部宏觀經濟環境變化資訊相關機制；明
reviewing the relevant mechanism on the collection of intelligence          確定義宏觀經濟風險分析環節；分析、檢討、調
about the changes in the external macro-economic environment;               整和改善未來的宏觀經濟風險應對策略。
clearly defining the macro-economic risk analysis session; analysing,

                                                   管理層討論及分析


MARKET OVERVIEW                                                            市場概況

In 2020, the Chinese and global economies were hammered by the             2020 年，新型冠狀病毒肺炎（「新冠肺炎」）疫情席
novel coronavirus pneumonia (“COVID-19”) pandemic, resulting in a          捲全球，中國以及環球經濟均遭遇重創，各種風

1.   CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION                                          1.   公司及集團資料

     The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in the                本公司為一間於開曼群島註冊成立的有限公
     Cayman Islands. The addresses of its registered office and                    司。其註冊辦事處及主要營業地點載於本報
     principal place of business are disclosed in the section headed               告「公司資料」部分。
     “Corporate Information” of this report.

     The Company is an investment holding company. Particulars of                  本公司為一間投資控股公司。本公司主要附
     the Company’s principal subsidiaries, associates and joint                    屬公司、聯營公司及合營企業的詳情分別載
     ventures are set out in notes 44, 17 and 18 to the consolidated               於綜合財務報表附註 44、17 及 18。
     financial statements, respectively.

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                      2.1 編製基準

     These consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                 本綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師公會所頒
     accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards                       佈的香港財務報告準則（「香港財務報告準
     (“HKFRSs”) (which include all Hong Kong Financial Reporting                   則」）
                                                                                     （包括所有香港財務報告準則、香港會
     Standards, Hong Kong Accounting Standards (“HKASs”) and                       計準則（「香港會計準則」）及詮釋）、香港公
     Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified               認會計原則及香港公司條例披露規定編製。
     Public Accountants, accounting principles generally accepted in               本綜合財務報表按歷史成本法編製，惟按公
     Hong Kong and the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong                    允價值計量的投資物業、其他金融資產及其
     Companies Ordinance. They have been prepared under the                        他金融負債除外。本綜合財務報表乃以人民
     historical cost convention, except for investment properties,                 幣呈列，除另有指明外，所有價值均調整至
     other financial assets and other financial liabilities which have             最接近的千元數。
     been measured at fair value. These consolidated financial
     statements are presented in Renminbi and all values are rounded
     to the nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.

     BASIS OF CONSOLIDATION                                                        綜合基準
     The consolidated financial statements include the financial                   綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司截至
     statements of the Company and its subsidiaries for the year                   2020 年 12 月 31 日止年度的財務報表。
     ended 31 December 2020.

     A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity), directly or        附屬公司為本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包
     indirectly controlled by the Company. Control is achieved when                括結構性實體）。當本集團承受或享有參與
     the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its             投資對象業務所得的可變回報，且能透過對
     involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect those             投資對象的權力（即賦予本集團現有能力主
     returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights            導投資對象相關活動的既存權利）影響該等
     that give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant                回報時，即取得控制權。
     activities of the investee).

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                      2.4 主要會計政策概要
    POLICIES

   INVESTMENTS IN ASSOCIATES AND JOINT                                       於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
   VENTURES
   An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term              聯營公司為本集團擁有一般不少於 20% 股本
   interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting rights       投票權的長期權益的實體，並可對其發揮重
   and over which it is in a position to exercise significant influence.     大影響力。重大影響力指參與投資對象的財
   Significant influence is the power to participate in the financial        政及經營決策之權力，而非控制或共同控制
   and operating policy decisions of the investee, but is not control        該等決策之權力。
   or joint control over those policies.

   A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the parties        合營企業為一項合營安排，據此，擁有安排
   that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net         之共同控制權的人士均有權享有合營企業的
   assets of the joint venture. Joint control is the contractually           淨資產。共同控制乃以合約協定分佔一項安
   agreed sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only            排之控制權，其僅在相關活動決策必須獲分
   when decisions about the relevant activities require unanimous            佔控制權的人士一致同意時方存在。
   consent of the parties sharing control.

   The Group’s investments in associates and a joint venture are             本集團於聯營公司及一間合營企業的投資乃
   stated in the consolidated statement of financial position at the         按權益會計法核算，按本集團應佔淨資產扣
   Group’s share of net assets under the equity method of                    除任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況表中呈列。
   accounting, less any impairment losses.

   The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other               本集團應佔聯營公司及合營企業收購後業績
   comprehensive income of associates and joint ventures is                  和其他全面收益分別計入損益及其他全面收
   included in profit or loss and other comprehensive income,                益內。此外，倘直接於聯營公司或合營企業
   respectively. In addition, when there has been a change                   的權益確認變動，則本集團會於綜合權益變
   recognised directly in the equity of the associates or joint              動表確認其應佔的任何變動（如適用）。本集
   ventures, the Group recognises its share of any changes, when             團與其聯營公司或合營企業進行交易而出現
   applicable, in the consolidated statement of changes in equity.           的未實現利益及虧損會按本集團於聯營公司
   Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions between           或合營企業的權益互相抵銷，但如果未實現
   the Group and its associates or joint ventures are eliminated to          虧損證明所轉讓資產發生減值則除外。收購
   the extent of the Group’s interests in the associates or joint            聯營公司或合營企業產生的商譽計入本集團
   ventures, except where unrealised losses provide evidence of an           於聯營公司或合營企業的投資的一部分。
   impairment of the asset transferred. Goodwill arising from the

2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                      2.4 主要會計政策概要（續）
    POLICIES (Continued)

   BUSINESS COMBINATIONS AND GOODWILL                                        業務合併及商譽
   Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition             業務合併乃以購買法入賬。轉讓之代價乃以
   method. The consideration transferred is measured at the                  收購日期的公允價值計算，該公允價值為本
   acquisition-date fair value which is the sum of the acquisition-          集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公允價值、本
   date fair values of assets transferred by the Group, liabilities          集團自被收購方之前度擁有人承擔的負債，
   assumed by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree and             及本集團發行以換取被收購方控制權的股本
   the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for control          權益的總和。於各業務合併中，本集團選擇
   of the acquiree. For each business combination, the Group elects          是否以公允價值或被收購方可識別資產淨值
   whether to measure the non-controlling interests in the acquiree          的應佔比例，計算屬現時擁有權權益並賦予
   that are present ownership interests and entitle their holders to a       其持有人有權於清盤時按比例分佔實體的淨
   proportionate share of net assets in the event of liquidation             資產之被收購方的非控制權益。非控制權益
   either at fair value or at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s      的一切其他成分乃按公允價值計量。收購相
   identifiable net assets. All other components of non-controlling          關成本於產生時列為開支。
   interests are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are
   expensed as incurred.

   The Group determines that it has acquired a business when the             當所取得的一組活動及資產包括一項投入和
   acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and a             一項重要過程，而兩者共同對形成產出的能
   substantive process that together significantly contribute to the         力有重大貢獻，則本集團釐定為業務收購。
   ability to create outputs.

   When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial             當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合約條
   assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and         款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條件，評
   designation in accordance with the contractual terms, economic            估將承接的金融資產及負債，以作出適當的
   circumstances and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date.        分類及標示，其中包括分離被收購方主合約
   This includes the separation of embedded derivatives in host              中的嵌入式衍生工具。
   contracts of the acquiree.

   If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously         倘業務合併分階段進行，以往持有的股本權
   held equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition-date fair value     益會以收購日期的公允價值重新計量，任何
   and any resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.           因此帶來的利益或虧損於損益中確認。

   Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer is         將由收購方轉讓的任何或然代價於收購日期
   recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent              按公允價值確認。或然代價（分類為資產或
   consideration classified as an asset or liability is measured at fair     負債）按公允價值計量，而公允價值變動則

   FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENT                                                    公允價值之計量
   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or        公允價值為市場參與者於計量日期在有序交
   paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between            易中出售資產所收取的價格或轉讓負債所支
   market participants at the measurement date. The fair value               付的價格。公允價值計量乃假設出售資產或
   measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction              轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要市場或（在
   to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the     無主要市場情況下）最具優勢市場進行而作
   principal market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a       出。主要或最具優勢市場須為本集團可進入
   principal market, in the most advantageous market for the asset           之市場。資產或負債的公允價值乃基於市場
   or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market must          參與者於資產或負債定價所用的假設計量（即
   be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability     假設市場參與者會以最佳經濟利益行事）。
   is measured using the assumptions that market participants
   would use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that
   market participants act in their economic best interest.

   A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into              非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及市場參與
   account a market participant’s ability to generate economic               者透過最大限度使用該資產達致最佳用途，
   benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by             或將該資產出售予將最大限度使用該資產達
   selling it to another market participant that would use the asset in      致最佳用途的其他市場參與者以產生經濟利
   its highest and best use.                                                 益的能力。

   The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the           本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備充分數據
   circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to              以供計量公允價值的估值方法，以盡量使用
   measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable             相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少使用不可觀
   inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                     察輸入數據。

   All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or            所有於本綜合財務報表計量或披露公允價值
   disclosed in the consolidated financial statements are                    的資產及負債乃基於對公允價值計量整體而
   categorised within the fair value hierarchy, described as follows,        言屬重大的最低層輸入數據按以下公允價值
   based on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value     層級分類：
   measurement as a whole:

   Level 1   –     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active             第一級   —   基於相同資產或負債於活躍
                   markets for identical assets or liabilities                         市場的報價（未經調整）

   Level 2   –     based on valuation techniques for which the               第二級   —   按估值方法計量，而對公允
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair                  價值計量而言屬重大的最低
                   value measurement is observable, either directly                    層輸入數據可直接或間接觀
                   or indirectly                                                       察得出

   Level 3   –     based on valuation techniques for which the               第三級   —   按估值方法計量，而對公允
                   lowest level input that is significant to the fair                  價值計量而言屬重大的最低
                   value measurement is unobservable                                   層輸入數據不可觀察得出

      inventories, contract assets, deferred tax assets and financial         產除外），則會就該項資產的可收回金額作
      assets), the asset’s recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s        出估計。資產的可收回金額為資產或現金產
      recoverable amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-                生單位的可使用價值與其公允價值減出售成

      asset does not generate cash inflows that are largely independent       其他資產或資產組別的現金流入，在此情況

      recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating unit           單位釐定。
      to which the asset belongs.

      an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value in           值虧損。於評估可使用價值時，估計未來現
      use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their             金流量將以除稅前貼現率折讓至現值，該貼
      present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current        現率反映現時市場對貨幣時間值及該項資產
      market assessments of the time value of money and the risks              的特定風險的評估。減值虧損將於其產生期
      specific to the asset. An impairment loss is charged to profit or        間於損益內與減值資產的功能一致的開支類
      loss in the period in which it arises in those expense categories        別中扣除。
      consistent with the function of the impaired asset.

      An assessment is made at the end of each reporting period as to          於各報告期末會評估是否有跡象顯示於過往
      whether there is an indication that previously recognised                確認的減值虧損可能不再存在或可能已減
      impairment losses may no longer exist or may have decreased. If          少。倘存在有關跡象，則會就有關可收回金
      such an indication exists, the recoverable amount is estimated. A        額作出估計。僅於用以釐定資產的可收回金
      previously recognised impairment loss of an asset other than             額的估計出現變動時，過往確認商譽以外的
      goodwill is reversed only if there has been a change in the              資產的減值虧損方可撥回，但撥回金額不得
      estimates used to determine the recoverable amount of that               高於假設過往年度並無就該資產確認減值虧
      asset, but not to an amount higher than the carrying amount that         損而應有之賬面值（扣除任何折舊╱攤銷）。
      would have been determined (net of any depreciation/                     該減值虧損的撥回於其產生期間計入損益內。
      amortisation) had no impairment loss been recognised for the

   currently undetermined future use and property that is being             業初步按成本（包括交易成本）計量。於初始
   constructed or developed for future use as investment property.          確認後，投資物業以反映於報告期末市況之
   Such properties are measured initially at cost, including                公允價值入賬。
   transaction costs. Subsequent to initial recognition, investment
   properties are stated at fair value, which reflects market
   conditions at the end of the reporting period.

   Gains or losses arising from changes in the fair values of               投資物業公允價值變動利益或虧損於產生之
   investment properties are included in profit or loss in the year in      年度計入損益。
   which they arise.

   Any gains or losses on the retirement or disposal of an investment       投資物業報廢或出售時產生的任何利益或虧
   property are recognised in profit or loss in the year of the             損於報廢或出售之年度於損益中確認。
   retirement or disposal.

   INVESTMENTS AND OTHER FINANCIAL ASSETS                                       投資及其他金融資產
   Initial recognition and measurement                                          初始確認及計量
   Financial assets are classified, at initial recognition, as                  金融資產於初始確認時分類為其後按攤銷成
   subsequently measured at amortised cost, FVTOCI and FVTPL.                   本計量、按公允價值計入其他全面收益及按
                                                                                公允價值計入損益。

   The classification of financial assets at initial recognition depends        於初始確認時，金融資產分類視乎金融資產
   on the financial asset’s contractual cash flow characteristics and           的合約現金流量特徵及本集團管理該等金融
   the Group’s business model for managing them. With the                       資產的業務模式而定。除並無包含重大融資
   exception of trade and bills receivables that do not contain a               成分的貿易應收款項及票據或本集團已就貿
   significant financing component or for which the Group has                   易應收款項及票據應用不調整重大融資成分
   applied the practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a             影響的可行權宜方法外，本集團初始按公允
   significant financing component, the Group initially measures a              價值（倘為並非按公允價值計入損益的金融
   financial asset at its fair value plus, in the case of a financial asset     資產則另加交易成本）計量金融資產。並無
   not at FVTPL, transaction costs. Trade and bills receivables that            包含重大融資成分或本集團已應用可行權宜
   do not contain a significant financing component or for which the            方法的貿易應收款項及票據根據香港財務報
   Group has applied the practical expedient are measured at the                告準則第 15 號（按照下文「收入確認」所載
   transaction price determined under HKFRS 15 in accordance with               的政策）釐定的交易價格計量。
   the policies set out for “Revenue recognition” below.

   In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at              為將金融資產按攤銷成本或按公允價值計入
   amortised cost or FVTOCI, it needs to give rise to cash flows that           其他全面收益進行分類及計量，金融資產需
   are solely payments of principal and interest (“SPPI”) on the                產生純粹為支付本金及未償還本金之利息
   principal amount outstanding. Financial assets with cash flows              （「純粹支付本金及利息」）的現金流量。現金
   that are not SPPI are classified and measured at FVTPL,                      流量並非純粹支付本金及利息之金融資產按
   irrespective of the business model.                                          公允價值計入損益進行分類及計量，而不論
                                                                                業務模式。

   The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers              本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其如何管
   to how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash             理金融資產以產生現金流量。業務模式釐定
   flows. The business model determines whether cash flows will                 現金流量會否產生自收取合約現金流量、出
   result from collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial         售金融資產，或兩者兼有。按攤銷成本進行
   assets, or both. Financial assets classified and measured at                 分類及計量的金融資產按持有金融資產以收
   amortised cost are held within a business model with the                     取合約現金流量的業務模式持有，而按公允
   objective to hold financial assets in order to collect contractual           價值計入其他全面收益進行分類及計量的金
   cash flows, while financial assets classified and measured at                融資產按旨在持有金融資產以收取合約現金
   FVTOCI are held within a business model with the objective of                流量及出售金融資產的業務模式持有。並非
   both holding to collect contractual cash flows and selling.                  按上述業務模式持有的金融資產為按公允價
   Financial assets which are not held within the aforementioned                值計入損益進行分類及計量。
   business models are classified and measured at FVTPL.

      All regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are         所有以日常買賣之金融資產，均按交易日（即

      impairment. Gains and losses are recognised in profit or loss       修訂或減值時，利益及虧損於損益確認。

      exchange revaluation and impairment losses or reversals are         而言，利息收益、外匯重估及減值虧損或撥
      recognised in profit or loss and computed in the same manner as     回於損益確認，並按與按攤銷成本計量的金

      or loss.

   IMPAIRMENT OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                              金融資產減值
   The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                本集團就並非按公允價值計入損益的所有債
   (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at FVTPL. ECLs are               務工具確認預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）
   based on the difference between the contractual cash flows due              撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合約應付的
   in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that the             合約現金流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金
   Group expects to receive, discounted at an approximation of the             流量之間的差額，並按接近原有實際利率貼
   original effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will              現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押品或
   include cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit         合約條款所包含的其他信貸增強措施所得的
   enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.                    現金流量。

   General approach                                                            一般方法
   ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which           預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初始確
   there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial      認以來信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸風險而
   recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from           言，會為未來 12 個月內的可能違約事件所產
   default events that are possible within the next 12 months (a               生的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧損撥備（12 個
   12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has               月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始確認以來信貸
   been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a     風險大幅增加的信貸風險而言，不論違約時
   loss allowance is required for credit losses expected over the              間如何，須就預期於風險餘下存續期內的信
   remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the           貸虧損計提虧損撥備（整個存續期預期信貸
   default (a lifetime ECL).                                                   虧損）。

   At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit               於各報告日期，本集團評估自初始確認後金
   risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since            融工具的信貸風險是否大幅增加。於進行評
   initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group                  估時，本集團將於報告日期金融工具發生違
   compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial                   約的風險與初始確認日期金融工具發生違約
   instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default              的風險進行比較，並考慮所有毋須付出不必
   occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial             要成本或努力即可獲得的合理支持資料，包
   recognition and considers reasonable and supportable                        括歷史及前瞻性資料。
   information that is available without undue cost or effort,
   including historical and forward-looking information.

   The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual           當合約付款已逾期 5 年，本集團認為金融資
   payments are 5 years past due. However, in certain cases, the               產將會違約。可是，於某些情況下，倘內部
   Group may also consider a financial asset to be in default when             或外部資料顯示在並無計及本集團任何信貸
   internal or external information indicates that the Group is                增強措施前，本集團不大可能悉數收到未償
   unlikely to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in full             還合約款項，本集團亦可能認為金融資產將
   before taking into account any credit enhancements held by the              會違約。於無法合理預期收回合約現金流量
   Group. A financial asset is written off when there is no reasonable         時撇銷金融資產。
   expectation of recovering the contractual cash flows.

      applies the practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a          收款項及票據和合約資產而言，本集團應用
      significant financing component, the Group applies the simplified         簡化方法計算預期信貸虧損。根據簡化方
      approach in calculating ECLs. Under the simplified approach, the          法，本集團並無追蹤信貸風險的變化，而於
      Group does not track changes in credit risk, but instead                  各報告日期根據整個存續期預期信貸虧損確
      recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs at each                認虧損撥備。本集團已根據其歷史信貸虧損
      reporting date. The Group has established a provision matrix that         經驗建立撥備矩陣，並就債務人及經濟環境

      forward-looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic

      Financial liabilities at amortised cost (loans and borrowings)          按攤銷成本列賬的金融負債（貸款及借款）
      After initial recognition, interest-bearing loans and borrowings        於初始確認後，計息貸款及借款其後以實際
      are subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective         利率（「實際利率」）法按攤銷成本計量。終止
      interest rate (“EIR”) method. Gains and losses are recognised in        確認負債及於實際利率攤銷過程中產生的利
      profit or loss when the liabilities are derecognised as well as         益及虧損於損益確認。
      through the EIR amortisation process.

      Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any discount        計算攤銷成本時會考慮收購所產生的任何折
      or premium on acquisition and fees or costs that are an integral        讓或溢價及構成實際利率組成部分的費用或
      part of the EIR. The EIR amortisation is included as finance costs      成本。實際利率攤銷計入於損益內的融資成
      in profit or loss.                                                      本。

      Convertible loans                                                       可換股貸款
      The component of convertible loans that exhibits characteristics        帶有負債特性的可換股貸款成分經扣減交易

      statement of financial position, net of transaction costs. On           發行可換股貸款時，負債成分的公允價值按

      non-convertible loan; and this amount is carried as a long term         換或贖回時獲償清為止。其餘所得款項經扣
      liability on the amortised cost basis until extinguished on             除交易成本後分配至獲確認的轉換權，並計
      conversion or redemption. The remainder of the proceeds is              入股東權益。轉換權的賬面值於其後年度不

      the liability and equity components of the convertible loans

      liability is discharged or cancelled or expires.                        終止確認。

      When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from        當現有金融負債由同一貸款人所提供條款差

      an existing liability are substantially modified, such an exchange      條款已作重大修訂，則有關取代或修訂被視

      the respective carrying amounts is recognised in profit or loss.

      PROVISIONS                                                               撥備
      A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or            倘因過往事件導致現有債務（法定或推定）及

      made of the amount of the obligation.

      required to settle the obligation. The increase in the discounted        會計入損益內的融資成本。
      present value amount arising from the passage of time is
      included in finance costs in profit or loss.

   recognised will not occur when the associated uncertainty with

   reflected in a separate financing transaction between the Group         集團提供超過一年重大融資利益的融資部分

   contains a financing component which provides the Group with a          法計算的合約負債所附加的利息開支。對於

   recognised under the contract includes the interest expense             為一年或不足一年的合約，交易價格不會採

            collect from the customers as reimbursement of costs and              作為原有建築合約中未包括的工程範
            margins for scope of works not included in the original               圍的成本及差額的補償。申索以可變
            construction contract. Claims are accounted for as variable           代價入賬並受其約束，直至其後與可
            consideration and constrained until it is highly probable             變代價相關的不確定性消除時確認的
            that a significant revenue reversal in the amount of                  累計收入金額極有可能不會發生收入
            cumulative revenue recognised will not occur when the                 大幅撥回。本集團使用預期價值法估
            associated uncertainty with the variable consideration is             計申索金額，乃由於此方法最有效預
            subsequently resolved. The Group uses the expected value              測本集團將有權獲得的可變代價金額。
            method to estimate the amounts of claims because this
            method best predicts the amount of variable consideration
            to which the Group will be entitled.

      (c)   Provision of sewage, hazardous waste treatment and              (c)   提供污水、危險廢品處理及相關環保

   FOREIGN CURRENCIES                                                     外幣
   The Company’s functional currency is Hong Kong dollars while           本公司的功能貨幣為港元，而本公司綜合財
   the Company’s presentation currency for the consolidated               務報表的呈列貨幣則為人民幣。於編製個別
   financial statements is Renminbi. In preparing the financial           集團實體之財務報表時，以該實體功能貨幣
   statements of each individual group entity, transactions in            以外貨幣（外幣）進行之交易，按交易當日適
   currencies other than the entity’s functional currency (foreign        用的匯率確認。於各報告期末，以外幣列值
   currencies) are recognised at the rates of exchange prevailing at      之貨幣項目按當日適用的匯率重新換算。以
   the dates of the transactions. At the end of each reporting            外幣按公允價值計量之非貨幣項目，按釐定
   period, monetary items denominated in foreign currencies are           公允價值當日之匯率重新換算。以外幣按歷
   retranslated at the rates prevailing at that date. Non-monetary        史成本計算之非貨幣項目不會重新換算。
   items carried at fair value that are denominated in foreign
   currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing at the date
   when the fair value was determined. Non-monetary items that are
   measured in terms of historical cost in a foreign currency are not
   retranslated.

   Exchange differences on monetary items are recognised in profit        貨幣項目之匯兌差額乃於產生期間內於損益
   or loss in the period in which they arise except for:                  確認，惟以下各項例外：

        relating to assets under construction for future productive            幣借貸匯兌差額被視為外幣借貸之利
        use, which are included in the cost of those assets when               息成本之調整時，匯兌差額計入該等
        they are regarded as an adjustment to interest costs on                資產之成本；
        those foreign currency borrowings;

   •    exchange differences on transactions entered into in order        •    對沖若干外幣風險交易之匯兌差額；
        to hedge certain foreign currency risks; and                           及

   •    exchange differences on monetary items receivable from or         •    應收或應付一項海外業務之貨幣項目
        payable to a foreign operation for which settlement is                 匯兌差額，既無計劃結算，發生結算
        neither planned nor likely to occur (therefore forming part            之可能性亦不大，其為海外業務投資
        of the net investment in the foreign operation), which are             淨額之一部分，並初始於其他全面收
        recognised initially in other comprehensive income and                 益確認及於償還貨幣項目時由權益重
        reclassified from equity to profit or loss on repayment of             新分類至損益。
        the monetary items.

3.    SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS                                       3.   主要會計判斷及估計
      AND ESTIMATES

      The preparation of the Group’s consolidated financial statements             編製本集團的綜合財務報表需要管理層作出
      requires management to make judgements, estimates and                        判斷、估計及假設，而該等判斷、估計及假
      assumptions that affect the reported amounts of revenues,                    設影響收入、開支、資產及負債的呈報金額
      expenses, assets and liabilities and their accompanying                      及相關披露，以及或然負債的披露。此等假
      disclosures, and the disclosure of contingent liabilities.                   設及估計的不明朗因素可引致日後需對受影
      Uncertainty about these assumptions and estimates could result               響的資產或負債的賬面值作出重大調整。
      in outcomes that could require a material adjustment to the
      carrying amounts of the assets or liabilities affected in the future.

      JUDGEMENT                                                                    判斷
      Deferred tax liabilities                                                     遞延稅項負債
      Deferred income tax liabilities have not been established for                遞延所得稅負債並無就以股息形式匯出及分
      income tax and withholding tax that would be payable on certain              派的中國附屬公司若干溢利的應付所得稅及
      profits of the subsidiaries in the PRC to be repatriated and                 預提所得稅計提，原因是董事認為撥回相關
      distributed by way of dividends as the directors consider that the           暫時差額的時間可被控制以及有關暫時差額

     Provision of ECL for contract assets, trade and bills                       提供合約資產、貿易應收款項及票據、其他

     The Group uses a provision matrix to calculate ECLs for contract            本集團使用撥備矩陣計算合約資產、貿易應
     assets, trade and bills receivables, other receivables and loan             收款項及票據、其他應收款項及應收借款的
     receivables. The provision rates are based on the invoice date for          預期信貸虧損。撥備率就有類似虧損模式的
     groupings of various customer segments that have similar loss               各客戶分部組別（即按地區、產品類型、客
     patterns (i.e., by geography, product type, customer type and               戶類型及評級，以及信用證及其他形式的信
     rating, and coverage by letters of credit and other forms of credit         用保險）的發票日期釐定。
     insurance).

     The provision matrix is initially based on the Group’s historical           撥備矩陣最初按本集團歷史觀察違約比率釐
     observed default rates. The Group will calibrate the matrix to              定。本集團將校準矩陣，以使用前瞻性資料
     adjust the historical credit loss experience with forward-looking           調整歷史信貸虧損經驗。例如，倘預測經濟
     information. For instance, if forecast economic conditions (i.e.,           狀況（即國內生產總值）於來年惡化而可能導
     gross domestic products) are expected to deteriorate over the               致製造業違約數量增加，則調整歷史違約比
     next year which can lead to an increased number of defaults in              率。於各報告日期，本集團更新歷史觀察違
     the manufacturing sector, the historical default rates are adjusted.        約比率並分析前瞻性估計的變動。
     At each reporting date, the historical observed default rates are
     updated and changes in the forward-looking estimates are
     analysed.

     Fair value of investment properties                                            投資物業公允價值

     (or subject to different leases or other contracts), adjusted to               並加以調整以反映有關差異；(iii) 類似物業

     Leases — Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                             租賃 — 估算增量借款利率
     The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a             本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利率，
     lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”)            因此使用增量借款利率（「增量借款利率」）計
     to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the         量租賃負債。增量借款利率為本集團於類似
     Group would have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with                經濟環境中為取得與使用權資產價值相近之
     a similar security, the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a                資產，而以類似借期與類似抵押品借入所需
     similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic                  資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款利率反
     environment. The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would                  映了本集團「應支付」的款項，當無可觀察
     have to pay”, which requires estimation when no observable                     的利率時（如就並無訂立融資交易之附屬公
     rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that do not enter into           司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以反映租賃
     financing transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to reflect             之條款及條件時（如當租賃並非以附屬公司
     the terms and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases                之功能貨幣訂立時），則須對利率作出估計。
     are not in the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group                    當可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）可用時，本
     estimates the IBR using observable inputs (such as market                      集團用此估算增量借款利率，並須作出若干
     interest rates) when available and is required to make certain                 實體特定的估計（如附屬公司的獨立信貸評
     entity-specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s stand-alone                級）。
     credit rating).

     Performance obligations                                                    履約責任
     Information about the Group’s performance obligations is                   有關本集團履約責任的資料概述如下：
     summarised below:

     Sale of goods                                                              銷售貨品
     The performance obligation is satisfied upon delivery of the               本集團於交貨時履行責任，付款一般在交貨
     goods and payment is generally due within 30 to 360 days from              後 30 至 360 天內到期，惟新客戶一般須預先
     delivery, except for new customers, where payment in advance is            付款。
     normally required.

     Renovation, installation and engineering services                          裝修、安裝及工程服務
     The performance obligation is satisfied over time as services are          履約責任隨提供服務而隨時間推移履行，且
     rendered and payment is generally due within 30 days from the              付款一般於結算日起 30 天內到期。客戶保留
     date of billing. A certain percentage of payment is retained by            一定百分比的付款直至保留期結束，乃由於
     customers until the end of the retention period as the Group’s             本集團獲取最終付款的權利視乎於客戶按合
     entitlement to the final payment is conditional on the satisfaction        約規定的一段時間內對服務質素表示滿意後
     of the service quality by the customers over a certain period as           方可作實。
     stipulated in the contracts.

     The amounts of transaction prices allocated to the remaining               於報告期末，分配至餘下履約責任（未履約
     performance obligations (unsatisfied or partially unsatisfied) at          或部分未履約）的交易價格的金額如下：
     the end of the reporting period are as follows:

                                                                                              2020            2019
                                                                                           RMB’000         RMB’000
                                                                                          人民幣千元           人民幣千元
     Amounts expected to be recognised               預期將確認為收入的
      as revenue:                                     金額：
      Not more than 1 year                            不多於 1 年                                954,379            778,063
      Over 1 year                                     1 年以上                                1,368,927            704,246

     The amounts of transaction prices allocated to the remaining               分配至預期將於一年以上確認的餘下履約責
     performance obligations which are expected to be recognised                任的交易價格的金額涉及裝修、安裝及工程
     over one year relate to renovation, installation and engineering           服務。所有其他分配至餘下履約責任的交易
     services. All the other amounts of transaction prices allocated to         價格的金額預期將在不多於一年確認。以上
     the remaining performance obligations are expected to be                   披露的金額不包括受約束的可變代價。
     recognised not more than one year. The amounts disclosed

13. LEASES                                                                  13. 租賃

      THE GROUP AS A LESSEE                                                   本集團作為承租人
      The Group has lease contracts for various items of leasehold            本集團擁有多個用於營運的租賃業權土地、
      land, plants and buildings, motor vehicles and other equipment          廠房及樓宇、汽車及其他設備的租賃合約。
      used in its operations. For leasehold land classified as prepaid        對於先前分類為預付土地租賃款的租賃業權
      land lease payments previously, lump sum payments were made             土地，向擁有人收購租賃期為 50 年的土地
      upfront to acquire the leasehold land from the owners with lease        時，已預先作出一次性付款，且根據該等土
      periods of 50 years, and no ongoing payments will be made               地租賃的條款，將不會進行後續付款。對於
      under the terms of these land leases. For other leased lands            先前分類為經營租賃的其他租賃土地，其租
      classified as operating lease previously, they generally have lease     期通常介乎 20 年至 50 年。廠房及樓宇租賃
      terms between 20 and 50 years. Lease of plants and buildings            的租期通常介乎 1 年至 10 年，而汽車及其他
      generally have lease terms between 1 and 10 years, while motor          設備的租期通常介乎1年至3年。一般而言，
      vehicles and other equipment generally have lease terms                 本集團被限制不能於本集團以外轉讓及轉租
      between 1 and 3 years. Generally, the Group is restricted from          租賃資產。本集團有幾份包含延期及終止權
      assigning and subleasing the leased assets outside the Group.           以及可變租賃付款的租賃合約，下文將作進
      There are several lease contracts that include extension and            一步討論。
      termination options and variable lease payments, which are
      further discussed below.

13. LEASES (Continued)                                                13. 租賃（續）

    THE GROUP AS A LESSOR                                               本集團作為出租人
    During the year, the Group leases certain commercial properties      年內，本集團於亞洲、北美洲及大洋洲租若
    in Asia, North America and Oceania which were classified as          干分類為投資物業的商業物業。年內該些物
    investment properties. The rental income of these properties         業已確認的租賃收益為人民幣 87,773,000 元
    amounting to RMB87,773,000 (2019: RMB76,847,000) was                （2019 年：人民幣 76,847,000 元）並計入損益
    recognised and included in “Revenue” in profit or loss. Certain      的「收入」內。若干辦公室、機器及設備（於
    office premises, machineries and equipment were classified as        經營租賃安排下）分類為物業、廠房及設備。

32. DEFERRED TAX (Continued)                                            32. 遞延稅項（續）

   Pursuant to the PRC Corporate Income Tax Law, a 10%                    根據中國企業所得稅法，在中國內地成立的
   withholding tax is levied on dividends declared to foreign             外資企業向外國投資者宣派的股息須繳納
   investors from the foreign investment enterprises established in       10% 的預提所得稅。該規定自 2008 年 1 月 1
   mainland China. The requirement is effective from 1 January 2008       日起生效，適用於自 2007 年 12 月 31 日起獲
   and applies to earnings after 31 December 2007. A lower                得的盈利。倘中國內地與該外國投資者所在
   withholding tax rate may be applied if there is a tax treaty           司法權區有簽訂稅務條約，則可按較低稅率
   between mainland China and the jurisdictions of the foreign            繳納預提所得稅。本集團的適用稅率為5%。
   investors. The applicable rate for the Group is 5%. The Group is       因此，就該等於中國內地成立的附屬公司就
   therefore liable for withholding taxes on dividends distributed by     2008 年 1 月 1 日起所得盈利而分派的股息而
   those subsidiaries established in mainland China in respect of         言，本集團須繳納預提所得稅。
   their earnings generated from 1 January 2008.

   At the end of the reporting period, the Group has not recognised       於報告期末，由於本公司控制該等附屬公司
   deferred tax liabilities of RMB346,198,000 (2019: RMB374,897,000)      的股息政策，且該等溢利於可見將來分派的
   in respect of temporary differences relating to the undistributed      可能性不大，故本集團並無確認該等附屬公
   profits of subsidiaries, amounting to RMB6,923,963,000 (2019:          司未分派溢利暫時差異為人民幣6,923,963,000
   RMB7,497,947,000), that would be payable on the distribution of        元（2019 年：人民幣 7,497,947,000 元）的遞延
   these retained profits as the Company controls the dividend            稅項負債為（須於分派該等保留溢利時支付）

42. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES                                       42. 金融風險管理目標及政策
    AND POLICIES

      The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise borrowings            本集團的主要金融工具包括借款以及現金及
      and cash and short-term deposits. The main purpose of these                短期存款。該等金融工具的主要用途乃為本
      financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s                  集團的營運籌集資金。本集團亦有其他由業
      operations. The Group has various other financial assets and               務直接產生的不同金融資產及負債，例如貿
      liabilities such as trade and other receivables and trade and other        易應收款項及其他應收款項和貿易應付款項
      payables, which arise directly from its operations.                        及其他應付款項。

      The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are          本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為利率風
      interest rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk, liquidity risk     險、外幣風險、信貸風險、流動資金風險及
      and equity price risk. As the Group’s exposure to these risks is           股本價格風險。由於本集團所承擔的該等風
      kept to a minimum, the Group has not used any derivatives and              險維持於最低水平，本集團並無使用任何衍
      other instruments for hedging purposes. The Group does not                 生工具及其他工具以作對沖之用。本集團並
      hold or issue derivative financial instruments for trading purposes.       無持有或發行衍生金融工具以作買賣之用。
      The Board reviews and agrees policies for managing each of                 董事會已審閱並同意該等風險管理的政策，
      these risks and they are summarised below.                                 茲概述如下。

      CREDIT RISK                                                             信貸風險
      Credit risk is the risk that a counterparty will not meet its           信貸風險指因交易對手無法履行其於金融工
      obligations under a financial instrument or customer contract,          具或客戶合約項下責任而蒙受財務虧損的風
      leading to a financial loss. The Group is exposed to credit risk in     險。本集團所面臨的信貸風險與其貿易應收
      relation to its trade and bills receivables, contract assets, loan      款項及票據、合約資產、應收借款、其他應
      receivables, other receivables, debt instruments at FVTOCI and          收款項、按公允價值計入其他全面收益的債
      cash and bank deposits.                                                 務工具以及現金及銀行存款有關。

      The concession operating right of the Group represent sewage            本集團的特許經營權指有關建造、營運及移
      treatment service concession financial receivables in respect of        交（「建造、營運及移交」）安排的特許污水處
      Build-operate-transfer (“BOT”) arrangements. Since the parties          理服務的金融應收款項。於報告期末，鑑於
      to BOT arrangements are local government authorities in the             建造、營運及移交安排的訂約方均為中國地
      PRC, the Group considers the credit risk is low at the end of the       方政府機關，故本集團認為信貸風險偏低。
      reporting period. The Group does not hold any collateral over           本集團並無就有關結餘持有任何抵押品。
      these balances.

      In relation to the Group’s financial services business, the Group       有關本集團的金融服務業務，本集團擁有信
      has a credit team which is responsible for the evaluation of            貸團隊負責評估客戶的信貸評級、財務背景
      customers’ credit ratings, financial background and repayment           及償還能力。本集團僅向信譽良好的客戶提
      abilities. The Group only provides credit to customers with             供信貸及一般須提供抵押品。該等客戶的付
      creditworthiness and collateral is normally required. The risk of       款拖欠風險並不重大。
      default in payment by these customers is considered not
      significant.

      The Group has policies in place to ensure that sales are made to        本集團訂有政策以確保向擁有穩健財政實力
      purchasers with an appropriate financial strength and appropriate       及可支付適當百分比之首期付款的買方作出
      percentage of down payments. It also has other monitoring               銷售，亦設有其他監控程序以確保採取跟進
      procedures to ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover          行動收回逾期債務。此外，本集團定期審閱
      overdue debts. In addition, the Group reviews regularly the             各項個別貿易應收款項及票據和合約資產的
      recoverable amount of each individual trade and bills receivables       可收回金額，以確保就不可收回金額作出足
      and contract assets to ensure that adequate impairment losses           夠的減值虧損。本集團的信貸風險源自多名
      are made for irrecoverable amounts. The Group has no significant        交易對手及客戶，故並無重大信貸風險集中
      concentrations of credit risk, with exposure spread over a large        的情況。
      number of counterparties and customers.

      For other receivables, an impairment analysis is performed by           就其他應收款項而言，管理層透過考慮歷史
      management at the end of each reporting period by considering           結算記錄、過往經驗及將來經濟狀況於各報
      historical settlement records, past experience and future               告期末進行減值分析。本公司董事認為，本
      economic conditions. The directors of the Company believe that          集團其他應收款項的未償還結餘並無重大固
      there is no material credit risk inherent in the Group’s                有信貸風險。
      outstanding balance of other receivables.

   CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                      資本管理
   The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to         本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本集團繼
   safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as going concern and          續以持續基準經營的能力，以及維持合理的
   to maintain reasonable capital ratios in order to support its           資本比率支持其業務，以及將股東的價值提
   business and maximise shareholders’ value.                              升至最高。

   The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments           本集團根據經濟狀況變動管理其資本架構及
   to it in light of changes in economic conditions. In order to           作出調整。為優化債務及權益結餘，本集團
   optimise the debt and equity balance, the Group may adjust the          可調整支付予股東的股息、向股東退回資
   dividend payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders,       本、發 行 新 股 或 新 債 或 贖 回 現 有 債 務。年
   issue new shares or new debts or the redemption of existing             內，管理資本的目標、政策或過程並無變動。
   debts. No changes were made in the objectives, policies or
   processes for managing capital during the year.

   The Group monitors capital using a net debt to equity ratio,            本集團以債務淨額對權益比率監控資本，該
   which is net debt divided by capital. Net debt includes                 比率為債務淨額除以資本。債務淨額包括借
   borrowings, convertible loans and lease liabilities less cash and       款、可換股貸款及租賃負債減現金及銀行存
   bank deposits. Capital represents the total equity.                     款。資本乃指權益總額。

   The Group’s strategy is to maintain the net debt to equity ratio at     本集團的策略為維持債務淨額對權益比率在
   a healthy level in order to support its businesses. The principal       健康水平，以支持其業務。本集團採取的主
   strategies adopted by the Group include, without limitation,            要策略包括但不限於審閱未來現金流量需求
   reviewing future cash flow requirements and the ability to meet         及支付到期債務的能力，保持可用銀行融資
   debt repayment schedules when they fall due, maintaining a              在合理水平及調整投資計劃及融資計劃（如
   reasonable level of available banking facilities and adjusting          需要），以確保本集團擁有合理水平的資本
   investment plans and financing plans, if necessary, to ensure that      支持其業務。於報告期末的債務淨額對權益
   the Group has a reasonable level of capital to support its              比率如下：
   business. The net debt to equity ratios, at the end of the
